457:def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
458:             once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
459:    """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
460:    #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
461:    whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
462:    if not marked or not arming(early, full):
463:        return "unarmed"
464:    if not alive:
465:        return "dead"
466:    cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
467:    if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
468:        return "nofire"
469:    got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
470:    if pin_if:
471:        got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
472:    if sp_at is not None:
473:        v = dumped(late, sp_at)
474:        #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
475:        #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
476:        if v == BOOT_SP:
477:            got += " sp"
478:        elif v == 0:
479:            got += " sp0"
480:    t = early + late
481:    if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
482:        got += " once"
483:    if declines:
484:        for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
485:            if announced(t, key):
486:                got += " " + tag
811:    got = "a=%s b=%s msr%d pop%d dpi%d save%d load%d" % (
812:        _half(DEST + 4, DEST + 8), _half(DEST + 12, DEST + 16),
813:        announces(t, "msr_imm"), announces(t, "arm_pop"), announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"),
814:        announces(t, "arm_save_register_bank"), announces(t, "arm_load_register_bank"))
815:row("latch rpt", got, "a=13/0/sp b=14/3/sp msr1 pop1 dpi1 save1 load1",
816:    "Five latch sites x2 in ONE process: exactly one announce each, repair kept on visit 2.")
817:diag_unimpl("latch rpt", early + late)
818:
819:# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: src dpi / src pop
820:#  CLASS CLOSURE of label x latch x outcome x masked bits (see the header).  Per label and path,
821:#  ONE process, four chained restores, each read back:
822:#    enter the label with I|F set; SPSR := N|Z|0xdf; restore     -> ACCEPT, latch clear  (R1)
823:#    re-enter it with I|F CLEAR;  SPSR_c := 0xcf;   restore     -> DECLINE, latch clear (R2)
824:#                                                    restore     -> DECLINE, latch SET   (R3)
825:#                                 SPSR_c := 0xdf;   restore     -> ACCEPT, latch set    (R4)
826:#  DPI chains through `movs pc,r5' (lr is banked; r5 is not) with r5 += 0x40 per block; POP chains
827:#  through `ldmia r6!,{pc}^' over a table of block addresses.  Want R1 = R4 = c00000df and R2 = R3
828:#  = c00000c0|label (mode kept, N Z I F from the SPSR), and EXACTLY ONE announce from the site.
829:BLK = [TGT + 0x40 * i for i in range(4)]     # 0x8100 0x8140 0x8180 0x81c0
830:for lab, mode in (("fiq", 0x11), ("irq", 0x12), ("abt", 0x17), ("und", 0x1B), ("svc", 0x13)):
831:    ck = "c_%02x" % (0xC0 | mode)            # first entry: I|F SET (helper operand bits 7:6)
832:    cc = "c_%02x" % mode                     # re-entry: I|F CLEAR, so the declines' I/F (set in
833:                                             # the SPSR) can only have come from the SPSR
834:    for path in ("dpi", "pop"):
835:        if path == "dpi":
836:            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
837:                [ck, "sf_c0", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"],
838:                blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "add_r5_40", cc, "s_cf", "movs_pc_r5"]),
839:                        (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "add_r5_40", "movs_pc_r5"]),
840:                        (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "add_r5_40", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"]),
841:                        (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
842:                regs=["r5=0x%x" % BLK[0]],
843:                segments=(((), 21),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
844:            key = "dpi S-to-pc"
845:        else:
846:            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
847:                [ck, "sf_c0", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"],
848:                blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", cc, "s_cf", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
849:                        (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
850:                        (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
851:                        (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
852:                data={LDMW + 4 * i: BLK[i] for i in range(4)},
853:                regs=["r6=0x%x" % LDMW],
854:                segments=(((), 18),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
855:            key = "arm_pop"
856:        if not marked or not arming(early, full):
857:            got = "unarmed"
858:        elif not alive:
859:            got = "dead"
860:        else:
861:            vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16)]
862:            got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % v for v in vals) \
863:                + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, key)
864:        acc, dec = NZ | 0xDF, NZ | 0xC0 | mode
865:        row("src %s %s" % (path, lab), got, "%08x %08x %08x %08x n=1" % (acc, dec, dec, acc),
866:            "%s from %s: accept, decline, decline (latch set), accept; one announce."
867:            % (path.upper(), lab.upper()))
868:        diag_unimpl("src %s %s" % (path, lab), early + late)

VERDICT: DO-NOT-SHIP | 2 floor breaches

These are two independent failures of the detector’s stated contract, established statically. I verified HEAD `ef577140`; wrote nothing, built nothing, and ran no probe or gate.

**1 | Yes—two escaping families remain.**

**Helper projection widening:** replace `ARM_FLAG_MODE` with `0x3f` in [the helper’s switch](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm.c:531). Every driven helper operand has bit 5 clear, so every result remains unchanged. `0xcf`, `0xdf`, and `0xdX` set **bits 7:6**, not 7:5.

Including `ARM_FLAG_C` instead gives another escape, without involving the disclosed T/execution-state limitation: no helper operand sets C, but the altered helper would reject a modelled mode accompanied by C.

**Declined-restore flag provenance:** preserving old CPSR N—or Z—inside either decline body also leaves every row unchanged. R1 installs N/Z; the subsequent `MSR CPSR_c` preserves them. Consequently, both declines already have old N/Z equal to SPSR N/Z. Other decline sessions have both sources clear. See [the actual sequences](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:837).

My denominator was:

| Static sweep | Shapes | Result |
|---|---:|---|
| Helper projection: add one bit, positions 5–31 | 27 | 4 detectable; **23 escape** |
| Clear-mask narrowing: every proper submask of `0x1f`, at MSR/DPI/POP | 93 | 91 detectable; 2 equivalent |
| Restore guard: five labels × two latch states × two paths × force true/false | 40 | All detectable |
| Decline body: retain old N/Z/I/F, individually, on either path | 8 | I/F detectable; **four N/Z variants escape** |

For projection widening, the driven upper-bit union is `0xc00000c0`. Algebraically, `2^23−1` added-bit subsets remain invisible among `2^27−1` possible widenings. These are static predictions, **not newly measured scores**.

**2 | Low-mode mask narrowing and the pure guard product close; broader field closure does not.**

All 40 enumerated restore cells distinguish the promised guard forcing/skipping faults: accepted destination SYS differs from every source label, and declined `0x0f` differs from every retained label.

But execution of a cell does not establish which source supplied its flags:

| Restore | Old CPSR flags set | SPSR flags set |
|---|---|---|
| R1 accept, latch clear | I/F | N/Z/I/F |
| R2 decline, latch clear | N/Z | N/Z/I/F |
| R3 decline, latch set | N/Z/I/F | N/Z/I/F |
| R4 accept, latch set | N/Z/I/F | N/Z/I/F |

Thus every latch-set decline lacks N/Z/I/F source discrimination. Across **80 declined flag cells**—five labels × two latches × two paths × four flags—only 20 distinguish old versus SPSR: I/F at R2. The other 60 do not.

Also, the question’s `~0x1b` example reverses the relationship: bit-2 leakage is visible at **FIQ, IRQ, UND and SVC**. UND is sensitive only to bit 2; bit 2 is not visible only at UND. The rows cover that shape despite its not having been built.

**3 | Backstop comments: yes. Contract and closure records: no.**

The rewritten comments agree with all four caller families: MSR, POP, DPI and exception entry. POP stops an unmodelled current mode before Save; DPI alone can reach Load while retaining the planted mode.

The listed NOT CLAIMED limitations are consistent with the probe, but they do not disclose the N/Z provenance hole or general undriven projection bits. Two affirmative claims remain false:

- [“bits 7:5 set”](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:81).
- [Old CPSR N/Z/I/F “CLEAR” before declines](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:100), including PRETABLE3’s assertion that I/F sources disagree in every decline cell.

**4 | Disposition 9 is wrong insofar as it declares the revised contract true.**

Disposition 1 correctly kills **`0xff` specifically**, but does not close projection widening. Disposition 4 correctly kills the measured forced-clear-I faults; its explanation overstates repeated-decline source discrimination. I found no basis to reject dispositions 2, 3, 5–8 or the reported 65 mutant results.

The additional measurements worth naming are **helper projection including C**, and **decline-only retention of old N separately in DPI and POP**. They directly test the two surviving families above.
